# Gas-liquid equilibrium parameterisations -- primary-review dossier (D6)

Generated by `bin/curate/interim_review_dossier.py` -- regenerate, never
hand-edit.  NOTHING in this dossier promotes a record: promotion of a
pending record is Vitor's primary-review decision alone, and a record
whose `reviewStatus` is `interim` is not promotable by definition.

Reading rules (ratified 2026-07-26): a divergence between INDEPENDENT
primaries is a curation FINDING, not an error; contradicted equivalence
(same source disagreeing) is the only error (gate-enforced); a point
outside a record's declared validity argues nothing about the record
inside its domain; one-within vs one-outside grounds a reasoned
recommendation, never an average; more coefficients is not better --
weigh source, experimental quality, domain, fitted variables, pressure,
temperature, convention, fit purpose, status.

## Coverage -- disjoint categories

Totals: **488 unique gas-liquid families** ((gas, solvent) after
typed normalisation), **495 parameterisations**
(486 H_xp pair records + 9 network gas-liquid records).
A physical family and a parameterisation are NOT the same count.

* **A. directly comparable** (same typed gas, dissolved identity and solvent -- token-equal or equivalent through a declared mapping): 6
    * CH4(g) -> CH4 in water: `parameters/Henry/CH4-water.dat` vs `chemistry/CH4-dissolution.dat`
    * CO2(g) -> CO2 in water: `parameters/Henry/CO2-water.dat` vs `chemistry/CO2-dissolution.dat`
    * H2(g) -> H2 in water: `parameters/Henry/H2-water.dat` vs `chemistry/H2-dissolution.dat`
    * N2(g) -> N2 in water: `parameters/Henry/N2-water.dat` vs `chemistry/N2-dissolution.dat`
    * NH3(g) -> NH3 in water: `parameters/Henry/NH3-water.dat` vs `chemistry/NH3-dissolution.dat`
    * O2(g) -> O2 in water: `parameters/Henry/O2-water.dat` vs `chemistry/O2-dissolution.dat`
* **B. both homes exist, equilibrium identities differ**: 1
    * H2S in water: `parameters/Henry/H2S-water.dat` (H2S) vs `chemistry/H2S-dissolution.dat` (H+ + HS-)
        * composite/fused equilibrium (gas dissolution + aqueous reaction in one constant): direct Henry comparison unavailable; a future comparison requires explicit thermodynamic decomposition through the molecular species' formation equilibrium, never labels
* **C. only the H_xp (Sander-convention) pair**: 479
* **D. only a PHREEQC/network equilibrium**: 2
    * C2H4O2(g) -> CH3COOH in water (Henry / infinite-dilution solute)
    * water(g) -> H2O in water (condensable solvent vapour equilibrium)
* **E. identity unresolved or ambiguous**: 0

Routes represented (a family's physical situation, NOT all 'Henry'):
* Henry / infinite-dilution solute: 484 parameterisation(s)
* composite gas-liquid + aqueous reaction: 1 parameterisation(s)
* condensable solvent vapour equilibrium: 1 parameterisation(s)
* reactive molecular gas-liquid equilibrium: 9 parameterisation(s)

## Cross-convention T-scans (category A families)

H_xp [Pa]: the Sander pair by its own van't Hoff form; the PHREEQC
molal K(T) converted with the explicit auxiliaries (55.508 mol/kgw,
ideal gas, 1 atm).  Validity marks compare NUMERICALLY against the
declared domain with no hidden rounding (373.15 K is outside a
273-373 K Trange); the near-boundary label is a PRESENTATION note
within 5 K of a declared endpoint and changes no decision.
`domain undeclared` means exactly that the machine-readable validity
domain is MISSING from the record -- a curation debt, not a licence to
use the correlation everywhere.  The scan below is a **common scan
range**, NOT a common validity domain: no known-valid intersection
exists until the PHREEQC domains are curated.
